```python
        [{1, 3}, {4, 5}],
```

```python
def test_latency_summary_and_input_validation():
    assert latency_summary([0.1, 0.3]) == {
        "calls": 2,
        "total_seconds": pytest.approx(0.4),
        "mean_seconds": pytest.approx(0.2),
        "p50_seconds": pytest.approx(0.2),
        "p95_seconds": pytest.approx(0.29),
    }
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="same length"):
        candidate_retrieval_metrics([[1]], [], 2)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="positive"):
        candidate_retrieval_metrics([], [], 0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="finite"):
        latency_summary([float("nan")])
```

```python
def test_candidate_helpers_deduplicate_and_sample_deterministically():
    assert drop_seen([1, 1, 2, 3], {2}, 5) == [1, 3]
    segments = {0: "warm", 2: "warm", 1: "cold", 3: "cold"}
    first = sample_users(segments, 1, np.random.default_rng(7))
    second = sample_users(segments, 1, np.random.default_rng(7))
    assert first == second
    assert set(first) == {"cold", "sparse", "warm"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="positive"):
        sample_users(segments, 0, np.random.default_rng(0))
```

```python
def test_metrics_hits_at_different_ranks():
    hits = np.array([[1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0], [0, 0, 1], [0, 0, 0]], dtype=bool)
    m = ranking_metrics_at_k(hits, np.array([1, 1, 1, 1]), 3)
    assert m["precision@3"].tolist() == pytest.approx([1 / 3] * 3 + [0])

    assert m["hit_rate@3"].tolist() == [1, 1, 1, 0]
    assert m["mrr@3"].tolist() == pytest.approx([1, 1 / 2, 1 / 3, 0])
    assert m["ndcg@3"].tolist() == pytest.approx([1, D2, D3, 0])
    assert m["map@3"].tolist() == pytest.approx([1, 1 / 2, 1 / 3, 0])
```

```python
def test_padding_never_counts_as_a_hit_or_as_coverage(world):
    # u3's only recommendable item is 3; k=3 gives [3, -1, -1].  Column index -1 would be the LAST item (5):
    # make item 5 the target and check it is NOT credited.
    target = sp.csr_matrix(([1.0], ([3], [5])), shape=(6, 6))
    r = evaluate_recommender(PopularityMatrixRecommender().fit(world.train), world.train, target, [3])
    assert r.overall["hit_rate@3"] == 0.0 and r.overall["precision@3"] == 0.0
    assert r.catalog_coverage["catalog_coverage@3"] == pytest.approx(1 / 6)  # only item 3; -1 is not an item
```

```python
def test_target_item_seen_ratio(world):
    train = sp.csr_matrix(world.train[:, :5].toarray().astype(np.float32))  # model never saw item 5 ...
    train = sp.hstack([train, sp.csr_matrix((6, 1), dtype=np.float32)]).tocsr()
```

```python
def test_ranking_metrics_are_reported_by_target_item_familiarity(world):
    train = world.train.tolil(copy=True)
    train[:, 5] = 0  # Item 5 is absent from fit, while item 4 remains seen.
    train = train.tocsr()
    target = sp.csr_matrix(([1.0, 1.0], ([0, 1], [4, 5])), shape=(6, 6))
    result = evaluate_recommender(PopularityMatrixRecommender().fit(train), train, target, [2])
    assert result.by_item_segment["warm_item"]["num_users"] == 1
    assert result.by_item_segment["cold_item"]["num_users"] == 1
    assert "recall@2" in result.by_item_segment["warm_item"]
    assert "ndcg@2" in result.by_item_segment["cold_item"]
    assert "by_item_segment" in result.to_metrics(fit_seconds=0)
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_accepts_any_sparse_format_numpy_ints_and_ignores_stored_zeros_and_duplicates(world):
    model = PopularityMatrixRecommender().fit(world.train)
    base = evaluate_recommender(model, world.train, world.target, [3])
    assert evaluate_recommender(model, sp.coo_matrix(world.train), sp.coo_matrix(world.target), [np.int64(3)]).overall == base.overall
    # stored zero row for u5 + a duplicated entry must not create a phantom user nor double-count relevant items
    t = world.target.tocoo()
    noisy = sp.coo_matrix((np.append(t.data, [0.0, 1.0]), (np.append(t.row, [5, 0]), np.append(t.col, [0, 4]))), shape=t.shape)
    r = evaluate_recommender(model, world.train, noisy, [3])
    assert r.num_users["all"] == 5 and r.overall == base.overall
```

_Padding -1 should not be counted as a hit in ground truth._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_evaluate_recommender_padding_neg_one_not_counted_as_hit():
    """Padding -1 should not be counted as a hit in ground truth."""
    num_users = 5
    num_items = 10
    ks = [5]

    # Create mock recommender that returns -1 padding
    model = MockRecommender(num_items=num_items, pad_with_neg_one=True)

    # Create exclude matrix (fit data)
    exclude = sp.csr_matrix((num_users, num_items))

    # Create target matrix - only item 0 is relevant for all users
    target = sp.csr_matrix((num_users, num_items))
    target[0, 0] = 1  # User 0 has item 0 in target
    target[1, 0] = 1  # User 1 has item 0 in target
    target[2, 0] = 1  # User 2 has item 0 in target
    target[3, 0] = 1  # User 3 has item 0 in target
    target[4, 0] = 1  # User 4 has item 0 in target

    result = evaluate_recommender(model, exclude, target, ks)

    # With -1 padding, only real items should be counted
    # Since item 0 is in recommendations (index 0), it should be a hit
    # But -1 should not cause false hits on the last item (index 9)
    assert result.overall["hit_rate@5"] > 0  # Should have some hits
    assert result.overall["hit_rate@5"] <= 1.0
```

```python
def test_evaluate_recommender_padding_neg_one_not_in_coverage():
    """Padding -1 should not be counted in catalog coverage."""
    num_users = 5
    num_items = 10
    ks = [5]

    # Create mock recommender that returns -1 padding
    model = MockRecommender(num_items=num_items, pad_with_neg_one=True)

    exclude = sp.csr_matrix((num_users, num_items))
    target = sp.csr_matrix((num_users, num_items))
    target[0, 0] = 1

    result = evaluate_recommender(model, exclude, target, ks)

    # Coverage should not include -1 (non-existent item)
    # Only real items (0, 1, 2 in this case) should be counted
    coverage = result.catalog_coverage["catalog_coverage@5"]
    assert coverage <= 0.5  # At most 3 items out of 10
    assert coverage >= 0.0
```

```python
def test_evaluate_ranking_with_valid_data():
    """Should work correctly with valid data."""
    model = PopularityRecommender()
    interactions = pd.DataFrame({
        "user_idx": [0, 0, 1, 1],
        "item_idx": [0, 1, 0, 2],
        "timestamp": [1, 2, 3, 4]

    model.fit(interactions)

    result = evaluate_ranking(model, interactions, [5])

    assert "recall@5" in result
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw,expected", [
    ("Pok\u00e9mon\u00ae \u2013 keyboard\u2122", "Pokemon - keyboard"),
    ("Fran\u00e7ais caf\u00e9", "Francais cafe"),
    ("10\u00d720 USB-C", "10 20 USB-C"),
    ("\u65e5\u672c\u8a9e", ""),
    (" \t ", ""),
    ("quiet usb-c keyboard", "quiet usb-c keyboard"),
])
def test_ascii_fold_and_raw_contract(raw, expected):
    assert normalize_query_text(raw, "raw") == raw
    folded = normalize_query_text(raw, "ascii_fold")
    assert folded == expected
    assert folded.isascii()
    if folded:
        _tokenize(folded)
```

**Inputs** (fixtures / parameters)

```text
raw = 'Pokémon® – keyboard™'
expected = 'Pokemon - keyboard'
```

```text
    in : query = 'Pokémon® – keyboard™'
    in : mode = 'raw'
    out: 'Pokémon® – keyboard™'
[2] normalize_query_text
    in : query = 'Pokémon® – keyboard™'
    in : mode = 'ascii_fold'
    out: 'Pokemon - keyboard'
[3] _tokenize
    in : query = 'Pokemon - keyboard'
    out: ['pokemon', 'keyboard']
```


```python
def test_paired_bootstrap_determinism_and_zero_differences():
    zero=_paired([0.,0.,0.],np.random.default_rng(42),100)
    assert zero["ci95"]==[0.,0.]
    assert zero["unchanged"]==3
    a=_paired([.1,-.2,0.,.3],np.random.default_rng(42),100)
    b=_paired([.1,-.2,0.,.3],np.random.default_rng(42),100)
```

```python
def test_resume_atomic_prefix_and_export(tmp_path):
    directory = tmp_path / "work"
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        with RankingRunStore(directory, {"seed":42}) as store:
            store.append("candidates", [(0,{"user_idx":9}), (1,{"user_idx":7})])
            # Simulate a transaction killed between INSERT and commit.
            store.conn.execute("INSERT INTO records VALUES ('candidates',2,5,'{}')")
            store.conn.rollback()
            raise RuntimeError("interrupted")
    with RankingRunStore(directory, {"seed":42}, resume=True) as store:
        assert store.count("candidates") == 2
        store.append("candidates", [(2,{"user_idx":5})])
        store.export(["candidates"], tmp_path / "candidates.jsonl")
        assert [json.loads(s)["user_idx"] for s in (tmp_path/"candidates.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()] == [9,7,5]
        assert store.timing()["sessions"][0]["status"] == "interrupted"
```

```python
def test_rejects_mismatch_duplicate_holes_and_concurrent_writer(tmp_path):
    directory = tmp_path / "work"
    with RankingRunStore(directory, {"protocol":"ascii"}) as store:
        with pytest.raises((OSError,BlockingIOError)):
            RankingRunStore(directory, {"protocol":"ascii"}, resume=True)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="prefix"):
            store.append("candidates", [(1,{"user_idx":9})])
        store.append("candidates", [(0,{"user_idx":9})])
        with pytest.raises(Exception):
            store.append("candidates", [(1,{"user_idx":9})])
        assert store.count("candidates") == 1
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="identity mismatch"):
        RankingRunStore(directory, {"protocol":"raw"}, resume=True)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="checkpoint exists"):
        RankingRunStore(directory, {"protocol":"ascii"})
```

```python
def test_partial_export_does_not_replace_complete_file(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    with RankingRunStore(tmp_path/"work", {}) as store:
        store.append("candidates",[(0,{"user_idx":1})])
        dest = tmp_path / "candidates.jsonl"
        dest.write_text("previous complete export")
        def fail(*a,**k):
            yield 0,{"user_idx":1}
            raise RuntimeError("disk interrupted")
        monkeypatch.setattr(store,"rows",fail)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            store.export(["candidates"],dest)
        assert dest.read_text() == "previous complete export"
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[candidates]`  (0.9921s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/tools/evaluation/test_ranking_streaming.py::test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality[scored:]`  (0.6723s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interrupt_stage", ["candidates", "scored:"])
def test_uninterrupted_and_resume_produce_identical_quality(environment,monkeypatch,interrupt_stage):
    base,paths,items = environment
    reference = launch(monkeypatch,base,"reference")
    original = RankingRunStore.append
    def interrupt(self,stream,batch):
        original(self,stream,batch)
        if stream.startswith(interrupt_stage) and self.count(stream)==1:
            raise RuntimeError("simulated power loss after commit")
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"append",interrupt)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="power loss"):
            launch(patch,base,"resumed")
    result = launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    for name in ("candidate_lists.jsonl","scored.jsonl"):
        a=[json.loads(l) for l in (reference/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        b=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/name).read_text(encoding="utf8").splitlines()]
        for rows in (a,b):
            for row in rows:
                for field in ("source_latency_seconds","ranking_latency_seconds","fetch_latency_seconds"):
                    row.pop(field,None)
        assert a==b
    m=json.loads((result/"metrics.json").read_text())
    assert sum(v["semantic_errors"] for v in m["tool_health"].values())==0
    # The stream summary reproduces existing batch metric implementations.
    rows=[json.loads(l) for l in (result/"scored.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    for merge in script.MERGES:
        for segment in script.SEGMENTS:
            selected=[r for r in rows if r["merge"]==merge and r["segment"]==segment and r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]]
            old=script._ranking_metrics([[items[i] for i in r["ranked_ids"]] for r in selected],
                                       [set(r["targets_unseen_in_fit"]) for r in selected],[1,3],len(items))
            assert old["metrics"]==m["ranking"][merge]["baseline"]["per_segment"][segment]["metrics"]
    # A finished checkpoint is idempotent and does not publish exp_002.
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"resumed",resume=True)
    assert not (result.parent/"exp_002").exists()
```

```python
def test_resume_after_export_failure_skips_models(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    export = RankingRunStore.export
    def fail(self,streams,destination):
        export(self,streams,destination)
        if Path(destination).name == "scored.jsonl":
```

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_resume_after_publication_marker_failure(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    put = RankingRunStore.put
    def fail(self,key,value):
        if key == "final_experiment":
            raise RuntimeError("marker interrupted")
        return put(self,key,value)
    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
        patch.setattr(RankingRunStore,"put",fail)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError,match="marker interrupted"):
            launch(patch,base,"published")
    assert (base/"published_output"/"exp_001").exists()
    def no_finalize(*a,**k):
        raise AssertionError("already published experiment must be recovered, not republished")
    monkeypatch.setattr(script,"_finalize",no_finalize)
    launch(monkeypatch,base,"published",resume=True)
    assert not (base/"published_output"/"exp_002").exists()
```

```python
def test_failed_baseline_confirmation_stops_tuning(environment,monkeypatch):
    base,paths,items = environment
    source=launch(monkeypatch,base,"bad_source")
    path=source/"metrics.json"
    metrics=json.loads(path.read_text())
    metrics["ranking"]["union_rr"]["baseline"]["per_segment"]["cold"]["metrics"]["1"]["ndcg@1"] += .1
    path.write_text(json.dumps(metrics))
    original=script.BaselineScorer
    called=[]
    def record(*a,**k):
        called.append(k["popularity_weight"])
        return original(*a,**k)
    monkeypatch.setattr(script,"BaselineScorer",record)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="baseline confirmation failed"):
        launch(monkeypatch,base,"blocked_tuning",frozen=source,weights="0,1e-6")
    assert called==[1e-6]
    assert not (base/"blocked_tuning_output"/"exp_001").exists()
```
